import pandas as pd
import numpy as np
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
import seaborn as sns
from scipy import stats

# Set style for plots
plt.style.use('seaborn-v0_8-whitegrid')
sns.set_palette('colorblind')

# Load the dataset
data = pd.read_csv('/Users/Lordof44/Documents/Projects/Dataset_Analyse/Paper_manus/paper/Body Composition and Survival in Liver Patients/data/fv_export.csv')

# Map numeric survival to labels
data['Status'] = data['surv_status'].map({0: 'Survived', 1: 'Deceased'})

# Create output directory for figures
import os
os.makedirs('results/figures', exist_ok=True)
os.makedirs('results/tables', exist_ok=True)

# Demographics and clinical data analysis
def analyze_demographics():
    # Basic demographics table
    demographics = pd.DataFrame()
    
    # Overall statistics
    demographics['Overall'] = [
        f"{len(data)} (100%)",
        f"{sum(data['sex'] == 'M')} ({sum(data['sex'] == 'M')/len(data)*100:.1f}%)",
        f"{sum(data['sex'] == 'W')} ({sum(data['sex'] == 'W')/len(data)*100:.1f}%)",
        f"{data['age'].median():.1f} ({data['age'].quantile(0.25):.1f}-{data['age'].quantile(0.75):.1f})",
        f"{data['weight_kg'].median():.1f} ({data['weight_kg'].quantile(0.25):.1f}-{data['weight_kg'].quantile(0.75):.1f})",
        f"{data['height_m'].median():.2f} ({data['height_m'].quantile(0.25):.2f}-{data['height_m'].quantile(0.75):.2f})",
        f"{data['bmi_recalculated'].median():.1f} ({data['bmi_recalculated'].quantile(0.25):.1f}-{data['bmi_recalculated'].quantile(0.75):.1f})"
    ]
    
    # By survival status
    survived = data[data['surv_status'] == 0]
    deceased = data[data['surv_status'] == 1]
    
    demographics['Survived'] = [
        f"{len(survived)} ({len(survived)/len(data)*100:.1f}%)",
        f"{sum(survived['sex'] == 'M')} ({sum(survived['sex'] == 'M')/len(survived)*100:.1f}%)",
        f"{sum(survived['sex'] == 'W')} ({sum(survived['sex'] == 'W')/len(survived)*100:.1f}%)",
        f"{survived['age'].median():.1f} ({survived['age'].quantile(0.25):.1f}-{survived['age'].quantile(0.75):.1f})",
        f"{survived['weight_kg'].median():.1f} ({survived['weight_kg'].quantile(0.25):.1f}-{survived['weight_kg'].quantile(0.75):.1f})",
        f"{survived['height_m'].median():.2f} ({survived['height_m'].quantile(0.25):.2f}-{survived['height_m'].quantile(0.75):.2f})",
        f"{survived['bmi_recalculated'].median():.1f} ({survived['bmi_recalculated'].quantile(0.25):.1f}-{survived['bmi_recalculated'].quantile(0.75):.1f})"
    ]
    
    demographics['Deceased'] = [
        f"{len(deceased)} ({len(deceased)/len(data)*100:.1f}%)",
        f"{sum(deceased['sex'] == 'M')} ({sum(deceased['sex'] == 'M')/len(deceased)*100:.1f}%)",
        f"{sum(deceased['sex'] == 'W')} ({sum(deceased['sex'] == 'W')/len(deceased)*100:.1f}%)",
        f"{deceased['age'].median():.1f} ({deceased['age'].quantile(0.25):.1f}-{deceased['age'].quantile(0.75):.1f})",
        f"{deceased['weight_kg'].median():.1f} ({deceased['weight_kg'].quantile(0.25):.1f}-{deceased['weight_kg'].quantile(0.75):.1f})",
        f"{deceased['height_m'].median():.2f} ({deceased['height_m'].quantile(0.25):.2f}-{deceased['height_m'].quantile(0.75):.2f})",
        f"{deceased['bmi_recalculated'].median():.1f} ({deceased['bmi_recalculated'].quantile(0.25):.1f}-{deceased['bmi_recalculated'].quantile(0.75):.1f})"
    ]
    
    # P-values for comparison between survived and deceased
    p_values = []
    
    # Total count - no statistical test needed for total counts
    p_values.append("-")
    
    # Sex - use chi-square test
    chi2_result = stats.chi2_contingency([
        [sum(survived['sex'] == 'M'), sum(survived['sex'] == 'W')],
        [sum(deceased['sex'] == 'M'), sum(deceased['sex'] == 'W')]
    ])
    p_values.append(f"{chi2_result[1]:.3f}")
    
    # Add a placeholder for the second sex row
    p_values.append("-")
    
    # Continuous variables - use Mann-Whitney U test
    for var in ['age', 'weight_kg', 'height_m', 'bmi_recalculated']:
        mannwhitney_result = stats.mannwhitneyu(survived[var].dropna(), deceased[var].dropna())
        p_values.append(f"{mannwhitney_result.pvalue:.3f}")
    
    demographics['P-value'] = p_values
    
    # Set index
    demographics.index = [
        'Total patients', 
        'Male', 
        'Female', 
        'Age (years)', 
        'Weight (kg)', 
        'Height (m)', 
        'BMI (kg/m²)'
    ]
    
    return demographics

# Clinical characteristics
def analyze_clinical_data():
    clinical = pd.DataFrame()
    
    # Overall statistics
    clinical['Overall'] = [
        f"{data['lab_meld'].median()} ({data['lab_meld'].quantile(0.25)}-{data['lab_meld'].quantile(0.75)})",
        f"{sum(data['has_ascites'] == True)} ({sum(data['has_ascites'] == True)/len(data)*100:.1f}%)",
        f"{sum(data['has_varices'] == True)} ({sum(data['has_varices'] == True)/len(data)*100:.1f}%)",
        f"{sum(data['has_encephalopathy'] == True)} ({sum(data['has_encephalopathy'] == True)/len(data)*100:.1f}%)",
        f"{sum(data['has_splenomegaly'] == True)} ({sum(data['has_splenomegaly'] == True)/len(data)*100:.1f}%)"
    ]
    
    # By survival status
    survived = data[data['surv_status'] == 0]
    deceased = data[data['surv_status'] == 1]
    
    clinical['Survived'] = [
        f"{survived['lab_meld'].median()} ({survived['lab_meld'].quantile(0.25)}-{survived['lab_meld'].quantile(0.75)})",
        f"{sum(survived['has_ascites'] == True)} ({sum(survived['has_ascites'] == True)/len(survived)*100:.1f}%)",
        f"{sum(survived['has_varices'] == True)} ({sum(survived['has_varices'] == True)/len(survived)*100:.1f}%)",
        f"{sum(survived['has_encephalopathy'] == True)} ({sum(survived['has_encephalopathy'] == True)/len(survived)*100:.1f}%)",
        f"{sum(survived['has_splenomegaly'] == True)} ({sum(survived['has_splenomegaly'] == True)/len(survived)*100:.1f}%)"
    ]
    
    clinical['Deceased'] = [
        f"{deceased['lab_meld'].median()} ({deceased['lab_meld'].quantile(0.25)}-{deceased['lab_meld'].quantile(0.75)})",
        f"{sum(deceased['has_ascites'] == True)} ({sum(deceased['has_ascites'] == True)/len(deceased)*100:.1f}%)",
        f"{sum(deceased['has_varices'] == True)} ({sum(deceased['has_varices'] == True)/len(deceased)*100:.1f}%)",
        f"{sum(deceased['has_encephalopathy'] == True)} ({sum(deceased['has_encephalopathy'] == True)/len(deceased)*100:.1f}%)",
        f"{sum(deceased['has_splenomegaly'] == True)} ({sum(deceased['has_splenomegaly'] == True)/len(deceased)*100:.1f}%)"
    ]
    
    # P-values for comparison between survived and deceased
    p_values = []
    
    # MELD - use Mann-Whitney U test
    mannwhitney_result = stats.mannwhitneyu(survived['lab_meld'].dropna(), deceased['lab_meld'].dropna())
    p_values.append(f"{mannwhitney_result.pvalue:.3f}")
    
    # Binary variables - use Fisher's exact test
    for var in ['has_ascites', 'has_varices', 'has_encephalopathy', 'has_splenomegaly']:
        surv_true = sum(survived[var] == True)
        surv_not_true = sum(survived[var] != True)
        dec_true = sum(deceased[var] == True)
        dec_not_true = sum(deceased[var] != True)
        table = [
            [surv_true, surv_not_true],
            [dec_true, dec_not_true]
        ]
        _, p = stats.fisher_exact(table)
        p_str = f"{p:.3f}" if not np.isnan(p) else "-"
        p_values.append(p_str)
    
    clinical['P-value'] = p_values
    
    # Set index
    clinical.index = [
        'MELD score', 
        'Ascites', 
        'Esophageal varices', 
        'Hepatic encephalopathy', 
        'Splenomegaly'
    ]
    
    return clinical

# Diagnosis distribution
def analyze_diagnosis():
    # Count diagnoses
    diagnosis_counts = data['diagnosis_grouped'].value_counts()
    
    # Create a DataFrame for the table
    diagnosis_table = pd.DataFrame()
    
    # Overall counts and percentages
    diagnosis_table['Overall'] = [
        f"{count} ({count/len(data)*100:.1f}%)" 
        for count in diagnosis_counts
    ]
    
    # By survival status
    survived = data[data['surv_status'] == 0]
    deceased = data[data['surv_status'] == 1]
    
    # Survived counts and percentages
    survived_counts = survived['diagnosis_grouped'].value_counts()
    diagnosis_table['Survived'] = [
        f"{survived_counts.get(diagnosis, 0)} ({survived_counts.get(diagnosis, 0)/len(survived)*100:.1f}%)" 
        for diagnosis in diagnosis_counts.index
    ]
    
    # Deceased counts and percentages
    deceased_counts = deceased['diagnosis_grouped'].value_counts()
    diagnosis_table['Deceased'] = [
        f"{deceased_counts.get(diagnosis, 0)} ({deceased_counts.get(diagnosis, 0)/len(deceased)*100:.1f}%)" 
        for diagnosis in diagnosis_counts.index
    ]
    
    # P-values for comparison between survived and deceased
    # Using Fisher's exact test for each diagnosis vs. all others
    p_values = []
    for diagnosis in diagnosis_counts.index:
        table = [
            [sum(survived['diagnosis_grouped'] == diagnosis), 
             sum(survived['diagnosis_grouped'] != diagnosis)],
            [sum(deceased['diagnosis_grouped'] == diagnosis), 
             sum(deceased['diagnosis_grouped'] != diagnosis)]
        ]
        _, p = stats.fisher_exact(table)
        p_str = f"{p:.3f}" if not np.isnan(p) else "-"
        p_values.append(p_str)
    
    diagnosis_table['P-value'] = p_values
    
    # Set index
    diagnosis_table.index = diagnosis_counts.index
    
    return diagnosis_table

# Create visualizations
def create_visualizations():
    # Age distribution by survival status
    plt.figure(figsize=(7, 5))
    ax = sns.histplot(data=data, x='age', hue='Status', multiple='dodge', bins=15,
                      palette={'Survived':'#2ca02c','Deceased':'#d62728'}, hue_order=['Survived','Deceased'],
                      shrink=0.7, edgecolor='black')
    plt.title('Age Distribution by Survival Status', fontsize=14)
    plt.xlabel('Age (years)', fontsize=12)
    plt.ylabel('Count', fontsize=12)
    from matplotlib.patches import Patch
    # Proxy legend
    legend_handles = [
        Patch(facecolor='#2ca02c', edgecolor='black', label='Survived (green)'),
        Patch(facecolor='#d62728', edgecolor='black', label='Deceased (red)')
    ]
    ax.legend(handles=legend_handles, title='Status', loc='upper right', frameon=False)
    plt.tight_layout(rect=[0, 0, 1, 1])
    plt.savefig('results/figures/01_age_distribution.png', dpi=300, bbox_inches='tight')
    plt.close()
    
    # MELD score distribution by survival status
    plt.figure(figsize=(7, 5))
    ax = sns.histplot(data=data, x='lab_meld', hue='Status', multiple='dodge', bins=15,
                      palette={'Survived':'#2ca02c','Deceased':'#d62728'}, hue_order=['Survived','Deceased'],
                      shrink=0.7, edgecolor='black')
    plt.title('MELD Score Distribution by Survival Status', fontsize=14)
    plt.xlabel('MELD Score', fontsize=12)
    plt.ylabel('Count', fontsize=12)
    from matplotlib.patches import Patch
    legend_handles = [
        Patch(facecolor='#2ca02c', edgecolor='black', label='Survived (green)'),
        Patch(facecolor='#d62728', edgecolor='black', label='Deceased (red)')
    ]
    ax.legend(handles=legend_handles, title='Status', loc='upper right', frameon=False)
    plt.tight_layout(rect=[0, 0, 1, 1])
    plt.savefig('results/figures/02_meld_distribution.png', dpi=300, bbox_inches='tight')
    plt.close()
    
    # Diagnosis distribution
    plt.figure(figsize=(10, 6))
    diagnosis_counts = data['diagnosis_grouped'].value_counts().sort_values(ascending=False)
    top_diagnoses = diagnosis_counts.head(10).index

    # Filter for top 10 diagnoses
    diagnosis_data = data[data['diagnosis_grouped'].isin(top_diagnoses)]

    # Crosstab for diagnosis and survival
    ct = pd.crosstab(diagnosis_data['diagnosis_grouped'], diagnosis_data['Status'])
    ct.columns = ['Survived', 'Deceased']
    ct_pct = ct.div(ct.sum(axis=1), axis=0) * 100

    # Plot: compact bars, better font, legend
    ax = ct_pct.plot(
        kind='barh',
        stacked=True,
        width=0.6,
        figsize=(10, 6),
        color={'Survived':'#2ca02c','Deceased':'#d62728'},
        edgecolor='black',
        alpha=0.92
    )
    for spine in ax.spines.values():
        spine.set_visible(False)
    ax.set_title('Top 10 Diagnoses by Survival Status', fontsize=16, pad=15)
    ax.set_xlabel('Percentage (%)', fontsize=13)
    ax.set_ylabel('Diagnosis', fontsize=13)
    ax.tick_params(axis='y', labelsize=11)
    ax.tick_params(axis='x', labelsize=11)
    # Add count labels
    for i, (idx, row) in enumerate(ct.iterrows()):
        ax.text(2, i, f"n = {row.sum()}", va='center', fontsize=10, color='black')
    legend = ax.legend(
        title='Status',
        loc='center left',
        bbox_to_anchor=(1.0, 0.5),
        fontsize=11,
        title_fontsize=12,
        frameon=False,
        labels=['Survived (green)', 'Deceased (red)']
    )
    plt.subplots_adjust(bottom=0.18, right=0.80)
    plt.figtext(0.5, 0.01, 'Green: Survived, Red: Deceased. Diagnoses are sorted by frequency. n = absolute count.',
                wrap=True, horizontalalignment='center', fontsize=10, color='dimgray')
    plt.tight_layout(rect=[0, 0.06, 0.95, 1])
    plt.savefig('results/figures/03_diagnosis_distribution.png', dpi=300, bbox_inches='tight')
    plt.close()
    
    # BMI distribution by survival status
    plt.figure(figsize=(6, 4))
    ax = sns.boxplot(data=data, x='bmi_group', y='bmi_recalculated', hue='Status',
               hue_order=['Survived','Deceased'],
               palette={'Survived':'#2ca02c','Deceased':'#d62728'},
               order=['Underweight', 'Normal', 'Overweight', 'Obese'],
               width=0.3)
    plt.title('BMI Distribution by BMI Group and Survival Status', fontsize=14)
    plt.xlabel('BMI Group', fontsize=12)
    plt.ylabel('BMI (kg/m²)', fontsize=12)
    from matplotlib.patches import Patch
    legend_handles = [
        Patch(facecolor='#2ca02c', edgecolor='black', label='Survived (green)'),
        Patch(facecolor='#d62728', edgecolor='black', label='Deceased (red)')
    ]
    ax.legend(handles=legend_handles, title='Status', loc='upper left', frameon=False)
    plt.tight_layout(rect=[0, 0, 1, 1])
    plt.savefig('results/figures/04_bmi_distribution.png', dpi=300, bbox_inches='tight')
    plt.close()

# Grouped summary tables by specified variables
def analyze_grouped(group_vars, num_vars):
    os.makedirs('results/tables', exist_ok=True)
    for var in group_vars:
        unique_vals = data[var].unique()
        print(f"Processing group: {var}, unique values: {unique_vals}")
        # Проверка: если все значения NaN или пусто — пропустить
        if pd.isna(unique_vals).all() or len(unique_vals) == 0:
            print(f"[!] Пропускаю {var}: все значения NaN или пусто!")
            continue
        ct = pd.crosstab(data[var], data['Status'])
        if ct.empty:
            print(f"[!] Пропускаю {var}: crosstab пустой!")
            continue
        ct_pct = (ct.div(ct.sum(axis=1), axis=0)*100).round(1)
        summary = ct.astype(str) + ' (' + ct_pct.astype(int).astype(str) + '%)'
        for nv in num_vars:
            med = data.groupby(var)[nv].median().round(1)
            q1 = data.groupby(var)[nv].quantile(0.25).round(1)
            q3 = data.groupby(var)[nv].quantile(0.75).round(1)
            summary[f'{nv} med (IQR)'] = med.astype(str) + ' (' + q1.astype(str) + '-' + q3.astype(str) + ')'
        # Для var=='Status' записываем только long-format вместо wide
        if var == 'Status':
            summary_long = summary.T
            summary_long.to_csv(f'results/tables/summary_by_{var}.csv')
            print(f"[+] Сохранил long-format: results/tables/summary_by_{var}.csv")
        else:
            # Запись wide-таблицы для остальных группировок
            summary.to_csv(f'results/tables/summary_by_{var}.csv')

# Body composition and ratio variables
volume_vars = ['bone','muscle','sat','vat','imat','eat','pat','tat']
ratio_vars = ['VSR','IMAC','FSM_Ratio','FVM_Ratio','AMVI','AVATI','ASATI','AIMATI','ATATI','ABI']

# Define grouping and variables
group_vars = ['Status','sex','diagnosis_final','diagnosis_grouped','age_group','meld_group','bmi_group','year']
# Numeric variables including body composition and ratio metrics
num_vars = ['age','weight_kg','height_m','bmi_recalculated','lab_meld'] + volume_vars + ratio_vars
analyze_grouped(group_vars, num_vars)

# Run the analyses and save summary tables
demographics_table = analyze_demographics()
clinical_table = analyze_clinical_data()
diagnosis_table = analyze_diagnosis()
create_visualizations()
demographics_table.to_csv('results/tables/01_demographics_summary.csv')
clinical_table.to_csv('results/tables/02_clinical_summary.csv')
diagnosis_table.to_csv('results/tables/03_diagnosis_summary.csv')

# Print tables
print("Demographics Table:")
print(demographics_table)
print("\nClinical Characteristics Table:")
print(clinical_table)
print("\nDiagnosis Distribution Table:")
print(diagnosis_table)

# Автоматически генерировать medical markdown-таблицы для всех .csv
import os
os.system('python code/table_formatter.py')

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
import seaborn as sns
from matplotlib.gridspec import GridSpec
import matplotlib.patches as mpatches

# Set style
plt.style.use('seaborn-v0_8-whitegrid')
sns.set_context("paper", font_scale=1.2)
plt.rcParams['font.family'] = 'DejaVu Sans'  # Using a font that should be available

# Create figure with 5 panels (a-e)
fig = plt.figure(figsize=(15, 18))
gs = GridSpec(3, 2, figure=fig, height_ratios=[1, 1, 1.2])
ax1 = fig.add_subplot(gs[0, 0])
ax2 = fig.add_subplot(gs[0, 1])
ax3 = fig.add_subplot(gs[1, 0])
ax4 = fig.add_subplot(gs[1, 1])
ax5 = fig.add_subplot(gs[2, :])

# Panel A: Forest plot for IMAC across MELD score groups
meld_groups = ['Low MELD (≤15)', 'Medium MELD (16-25)', 'High MELD (>25)']
imac_hr = [14.97, 125.18, 19.38]
imac_lower_ci = [0.21, 2.65, 0.42]
imac_upper_ci = [1067.18, 5923.92, 901.68]
imac_p = [0.2139, 0.0141, 0.1302]

# For visualization purposes, cap the upper CI
for i in range(len(imac_upper_ci)):
    if imac_upper_ci[i] > 100:
        imac_upper_ci[i] = 100

# Calculate error bars - ensure no negative values
imac_lower_error = [max(0, hr - l) for hr, l in zip(imac_hr, imac_lower_ci)]
imac_upper_error = [max(0, min(u, 100) - hr) for hr, u in zip(imac_hr, imac_upper_ci)]
imac_asymmetric_error = [imac_lower_error, imac_upper_error]

# Create colors based on significance
imac_colors = ['#d3d3d3' if p >= 0.05 else '#1f77b4' for p in imac_p]

# Plot hazard ratios
y_pos = np.arange(len(meld_groups))
ax1.barh(y_pos, imac_hr, xerr=imac_asymmetric_error, align='center', color=imac_colors, alpha=0.7, height=0.6)
ax1.set_yticks(y_pos)
ax1.set_yticklabels(meld_groups)
ax1.set_xscale('log')
ax1.set_xlim(0.1, 150)
ax1.axvline(x=1, color='black', linestyle='--', alpha=0.7)
ax1.set_xlabel('Hazard Ratio (95% CI)')
ax1.set_title('a', loc='left', fontweight='bold', fontsize=14)
ax1.set_title('IMAC by MELD Score Groups', fontsize=12)

# Add p-values
for i, p in enumerate(imac_p):
    if p < 0.001:
        p_text = "p<0.001"
    elif p < 0.01:
        p_text = f"p={p:.3f}"
    elif p < 0.05:
        p_text = f"p={p:.3f}"
    else:
        p_text = f"p={p:.3f}"
    
    # Position the text at the end of the bar
    hr_val = min(imac_hr[i] + imac_upper_error[i], 140)
    ax1.text(hr_val * 1.1, i, p_text, va='center', fontsize=9)

# Panel B: Forest plot for FVM_Ratio across MELD score groups
fvm_hr = [4.07, 3.50, 3.22]
fvm_lower_ci = [1.30, 0.90, 0.68]
fvm_upper_ci = [12.80, 13.62, 15.36]
fvm_p = [0.0162, 0.0702, 0.1422]

# Calculate error bars - ensure no negative values
fvm_lower_error = [max(0, hr - l) for hr, l in zip(fvm_hr, fvm_lower_ci)]
fvm_upper_error = [max(0, u - hr) for hr, u in zip(fvm_hr, fvm_upper_ci)]
fvm_asymmetric_error = [fvm_lower_error, fvm_upper_error]

# Create colors based on significance
fvm_colors = ['#d3d3d3' if p >= 0.05 else '#1f77b4' for p in fvm_p]

# Plot hazard ratios
ax2.barh(y_pos, fvm_hr, xerr=fvm_asymmetric_error, align='center', color=fvm_colors, alpha=0.7, height=0.6)
ax2.set_yticks(y_pos)
ax2.set_yticklabels(meld_groups)
ax2.set_xscale('log')
ax2.set_xlim(0.1, 20)
ax2.axvline(x=1, color='black', linestyle='--', alpha=0.7)
ax2.set_xlabel('Hazard Ratio (95% CI)')
ax2.set_title('b', loc='left', fontweight='bold', fontsize=14)
ax2.set_title('FVM_Ratio by MELD Score Groups', fontsize=12)

# Add p-values
for i, p in enumerate(fvm_p):
    if p < 0.001:
        p_text = "p<0.001"
    elif p < 0.01:
        p_text = f"p={p:.3f}"
    elif p < 0.05:
        p_text = f"p={p:.3f}"
    else:
        p_text = f"p={p:.3f}"
    
    # Position the text at the end of the bar
    hr_val = min(fvm_hr[i] + fvm_upper_error[i], 18)
    ax2.text(hr_val * 1.1, i, p_text, va='center', fontsize=9)

# Panel C: Forest plot for IMAC across BMI categories
bmi_groups = ['Normal Weight', 'Overweight', 'Obese']
imac_bmi_hr = [349.28, 0.35, 20.03]
imac_bmi_lower_ci = [9.45, 0.00, 0.40]
imac_bmi_upper_ci = [12915.41, 99.12, 1014.65]
imac_bmi_p = [0.0015, 0.7185, 0.1344]

# For visualization purposes, cap the upper CI
for i in range(len(imac_bmi_upper_ci)):
    if imac_bmi_upper_ci[i] > 100:
        imac_bmi_upper_ci[i] = 100

# Calculate error bars - ensure no negative values
imac_bmi_lower_error = [max(0, hr - l) for hr, l in zip(imac_bmi_hr, imac_bmi_lower_ci)]
imac_bmi_upper_error = [max(0, min(u, 100) - hr) for hr, u in zip(imac_bmi_hr, imac_bmi_upper_ci)]

# Create colors based on significance
imac_bmi_colors = ['#d3d3d3' if p >= 0.05 else '#1f77b4' for p in imac_bmi_p]

# Plot hazard ratios
y_pos = np.arange(len(bmi_groups))
ax3.barh(y_pos, imac_bmi_hr, xerr=imac_bmi_asymmetric_error, align='center', color=imac_bmi_colors, alpha=0.7, height=0.6)
ax3.set_yticks(y_pos)
ax3.set_yticklabels(bmi_groups)
ax3.set_xscale('log')
ax3.set_xlim(0.01, 400)
ax3.axvline(x=1, color='black', linestyle='--', alpha=0.7)
ax3.set_xlabel('Hazard Ratio (95% CI)')
ax3.set_title('c', loc='left', fontweight='bold', fontsize=14)
ax3.set_title('IMAC by BMI Categories', fontsize=12)

# Add p-values
for i, p in enumerate(imac_bmi_p):
    if p < 0.001:
        p_text = "p<0.001"
    elif p < 0.01:
        p_text = f"p={p:.3f}"
    elif p < 0.05:
        p_text = f"p={p:.3f}"
    else:
        p_text = f"p={p:.3f}"
    
    # Position the text at the end of the bar
    hr_val = min(imac_bmi_hr[i] + imac_bmi_upper_error[i], 350)
    ax3.text(hr_val * 1.1, i, p_text, va='center', fontsize=9)

# Panel D: Forest plot for FVM_Ratio across BMI categories
fvm_bmi_hr = [2.59, 1.62, 2.93]
fvm_bmi_lower_ci = [0.67, 0.28, 0.89]
fvm_bmi_upper_ci = [10.02, 9.52, 9.65]
fvm_bmi_p = [0.1675, 0.5943, 0.0764]

# Calculate error bars - ensure no negative values
fvm_bmi_lower_error = [max(0, hr - l) for hr, l in zip(fvm_bmi_hr, fvm_bmi_lower_ci)]
fvm_bmi_upper_error = [max(0, u - hr) for hr, u in zip(fvm_bmi_hr, fvm_bmi_upper_ci)]
fvm_bmi_asymmetric_error = [fvm_bmi_lower_error, fvm_bmi_upper_error]

# Create colors based on significance
fvm_bmi_colors = ['#d3d3d3' if p >= 0.05 else '#1f77b4' for p in fvm_bmi_p]

# Plot hazard ratios
ax4.barh(y_pos, fvm_bmi_hr, xerr=fvm_bmi_asymmetric_error, align='center', color=fvm_bmi_colors, alpha=0.7, height=0.6)
ax4.set_yticks(y_pos)
ax4.set_yticklabels(bmi_groups)
ax4.set_xscale('log')
ax4.set_xlim(0.1, 15)
ax4.axvline(x=1, color='black', linestyle='--', alpha=0.7)
ax4.set_xlabel('Hazard Ratio (95% CI)')
ax4.set_title('d', loc='left', fontweight='bold', fontsize=14)
ax4.set_title('FVM_Ratio by BMI Categories', fontsize=12)

# Add p-values
for i, p in enumerate(fvm_bmi_p):
    if p < 0.001:
        p_text = "p<0.001"
    elif p < 0.01:
        p_text = f"p={p:.3f}"
    elif p < 0.05:
        p_text = f"p={p:.3f}"
    else:
        p_text = f"p={p:.3f}"
    
    # Position the text at the end of the bar
    hr_val = min(fvm_bmi_hr[i] + fvm_bmi_upper_error[i], 13)
    ax4.text(hr_val * 1.1, i, p_text, va='center', fontsize=9)

# Panel E: Heatmap of prognostic significance
# Create data for heatmap
parameters = ['IMAC', 'FVM_Ratio', 'FSM_Ratio', 'VSR', 'AMVI', 'Muscle', 'VAT', 'SAT']
groups = ['Low MELD', 'Medium MELD', 'High MELD', 'Normal Weight', 'Overweight', 'Obese']

# p-values for each parameter across groups
p_values = [
    [0.2139, 0.0141, 0.1302, 0.0015, 0.7185, 0.1344],  # IMAC
    [0.0162, 0.0702, 0.1422, 0.1675, 0.5943, 0.0764],  # FVM_Ratio
    [0.9835, 0.6001, 0.0388, 0.6301, 0.2337, 0.6669],  # FSM_Ratio
    [0.1253, 0.1139, 0.7007, 0.7580, 0.1327, 0.1420],  # VSR
    [0.0007, 0.2306, 0.0212, 0.6038, 0.0537, 0.0071],  # AMVI
    [0.0003, 0.3583, 0.0331, 0.6649, 0.0696, 0.0091],  # Muscle
    [0.0003, 0.0606, 0.0081, 0.2294, 0.2073, 0.0053],  # VAT
    [0.0553, 0.7324, 0.0011, 0.5454, 0.7128, 0.0708]   # SAT
]

# Convert to -log10(p) for better visualization
log_p_values = [[-np.log10(p) if p > 0 else 3 for p in row] for row in p_values]
log_p_df = pd.DataFrame(log_p_values, index=parameters, columns=groups)

# Create heatmap
cmap = sns.diverging_palette(240, 10, as_cmap=True)
sns.heatmap(log_p_df, cmap=cmap, center=0.7, vmin=0, vmax=3, 
            annot=True, fmt=".2f", linewidths=.5, ax=ax5)

ax5.set_title('e', loc='left', fontweight='bold', fontsize=14)
ax5.set_title('Prognostic Significance (-log10(p-value)) Across Subgroups', fontsize=12)

# Add significance threshold line in legend
significance_patch = mpatches.Patch(color=cmap(0.7), label='-log10(0.05) = 1.30 (significance threshold)')
ax5.legend(handles=[significance_patch], loc='upper right')

\`\`\`py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
import os
import subprocess

# Базовый класс агента
class Agent:
    def __init__(self, name):
        self.name = name
    def process(self, data):
        raise NotImplementedError

# 1. Nature: генерирует summary_by_period.csv
class Nature(Agent):
    def process(self, _):
        print(f"[{self.name}] Generating summary_by_period.csv...")
        subprocess.run(['python3', 'code/summary_by_period.py'], check=True)
        return os.path.join('results', 'tables', 'summary_by_period.csv')

# 2. CreativeKeyMaster: (фейковая логика, тут можно доработать препроцессинг)
class CreativeKeyMaster(Agent):
    def process(self, csv_path):
        print(f"[{self.name}] Preprocessing {csv_path}...")
        # например, проверка или вставка метаданных
        return csv_path

# 3. Reflector: запускает table_formatter и получает markdown
class Reflector(Agent):
    def process(self, csv_path):
        print(f"[{self.name}] Formatting markdown from {csv_path}...")
        subprocess.run(['python3', 'code/table_formatter.py'], check=True)
        return os.path.join('results', 'tables', 'summary_by_period.md')

# 4. InsightDeriver: финальный вывод и сообщение
class InsightDeriver(Agent):
    def process(self, md_path):
        print(f"[{self.name}] Pipeline completed, output at {md_path}")
        return md_path

# Main orchestration
if __name__ == '__main__':
    agents = [
        Nature('Nature'),
        CreativeKeyMaster('CreativeKeyMaster'),
        Reflector('Reflector'),
        InsightDeriver('InsightDeriver')
    ]
    x = None
    for agent in agents:
        x = agent.process(x)
    print(f"🚀 OUTCOME:\n{x}")

\`\`\`py
import pandas as pd
import numpy as np
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
import seaborn as sns
from scipy import stats
import os

# Set style for plots
plt.style.use('seaborn-v0_8-whitegrid')
sns.set_palette('colorblind')

# Load the dataset
data = pd.read_csv('/home/ubuntu/upload/fv_export.csv')

# Create output directory for figures
os.makedirs('/home/ubuntu/analysis/figures', exist_ok=True)

# Define body composition parameters
bc_params = ['bone', 'muscle', 'sat', 'vat', 'imat', 'eat', 'pat', 'tat', 
             'VSR', 'IMAC', 'FSM_Ratio', 'FVM_Ratio', 'AMVI', 'AVATI', 
             'ASATI', 'AIMATI', 'ATATI', 'ABI']

# Analyze body composition parameters
def analyze_bc_parameters():
    # Create DataFrame for BC parameters table
    bc_table = pd.DataFrame()
    
    # Overall statistics
    overall_stats = []
    for param in bc_params:
        median = data[param].median()
        q1 = data[param].quantile(0.25)
        q3 = data[param].quantile(0.75)
        overall_stats.append(f"{median:.2f} ({q1:.2f}-{q3:.2f})")
    
    bc_table['Overall'] = overall_stats
    
    # By survival status
    survived = data[data['surv_status'] == 0]
    deceased = data[data['surv_status'] == 1]
    
    # Survived statistics
    survived_stats = []
    for param in bc_params:
        median = survived[param].median()
        q1 = survived[param].quantile(0.25)
        q3 = survived[param].quantile(0.75)
        survived_stats.append(f"{median:.2f} ({q1:.2f}-{q3:.2f})")
    
    bc_table['Survived'] = survived_stats
    
    # Deceased statistics
    deceased_stats = []
    for param in bc_params:
        median = deceased[param].median()
        q1 = deceased[param].quantile(0.25)
        q3 = deceased[param].quantile(0.75)
        deceased_stats.append(f"{median:.2f} ({q1:.2f}-{q3:.2f})")
    
    bc_table['Deceased'] = deceased_stats
    
    # P-values for comparison between survived and deceased
    p_values = []
    for param in bc_params:
        mannwhitney_result = stats.mannwhitneyu(survived[param].dropna(), deceased[param].dropna())
        p_values.append(f"{mannwhitney_result.pvalue:.3f}")
    
    bc_table['P-value'] = p_values
    
    # Set index
    bc_table.index = bc_params
    
    return bc_table

# Create visualizations for body composition parameters
def create_bc_visualizations():
    # Key BC parameters to visualize
    key_params = ['FVM_Ratio', 'VSR', 'IMAC', 'AMVI']
    
    # Boxplots for key BC parameters by survival status
    for param in key_params:
        plt.figure(figsize=(10, 6))
        sns.boxplot(x='surv_status', y=param, data=data, palette=['#2ca02c', '#d62728'])
        plt.title(f'{param} by Survival Status', fontsize=14)
        plt.xlabel('Survival Status (0=Survived, 1=Deceased)', fontsize=12)
        plt.ylabel(param, fontsize=12)
        plt.tight_layout()
        plt.savefig(f'/home/ubuntu/analysis/figures/{param}_by_survival.png', dpi=300)
    
    # Correlation heatmap of BC parameters
    plt.figure(figsize=(14, 12))
    corr = data[bc_params].corr()
    mask = np.triu(np.ones_like(corr, dtype=bool))
    sns.heatmap(corr, mask=mask, cmap='coolwarm', annot=True, fmt='.2f', 
                square=True, linewidths=.5)
    plt.title('Correlation Between Body Composition Parameters', fontsize=16)
    plt.tight_layout()
    plt.savefig('/home/ubuntu/analysis/figures/bc_correlation_heatmap.png', dpi=300)
    
    # Distribution of key BC parameters by MELD group
    for param in key_params:
        plt.figure(figsize=(12, 8))
        sns.boxplot(x='meld_group', y=param, data=data, 
                   order=['Low MELD (≤15)', 'Medium MELD (16-25)', 'High MELD (>25)'])
        plt.title(f'{param} by MELD Score Group', fontsize=14)
        plt.xlabel('MELD Score Group', fontsize=12)
        plt.ylabel(param, fontsize=12)
        plt.tight_layout()
        plt.savefig(f'/home/ubuntu/analysis/figures/{param}_by_meld_group.png', dpi=300)
    
    # Distribution of key BC parameters by BMI group
    for param in key_params:
        plt.figure(figsize=(12, 8))
        sns.boxplot(x='bmi_group', y=param, data=data, 
                   order=['Underweight', 'Normal', 'Overweight', 'Obese'])
        plt.title(f'{param} by BMI Group', fontsize=14)
        plt.xlabel('BMI Group', fontsize=12)
        plt.ylabel(param, fontsize=12)
        plt.tight_layout()
        plt.savefig(f'/home/ubuntu/analysis/figures/{param}_by_bmi_group.png', dpi=300)

# Analyze BC parameters by sex
def analyze_bc_by_sex():
    # Create DataFrame for BC parameters by sex
    bc_sex_table = pd.DataFrame()
    
    # Male statistics
    male_data = data[data['sex'] == 'M']
    male_stats = []
    for param in bc_params:
        median = male_data[param].median()
        q1 = male_data[param].quantile(0.25)
        q3 = male_data[param].quantile(0.75)
        male_stats.append(f"{median:.2f} ({q1:.2f}-{q3:.2f})")
    
    bc_sex_table['Male'] = male_stats
    
    # Female statistics
    female_data = data[data['sex'] == 'W']
    female_stats = []
    for param in bc_params:
        median = female_data[param].median()
        q1 = female_data[param].quantile(0.25)
        q3 = female_data[param].quantile(0.75)
        female_stats.append(f"{median:.2f} ({q1:.2f}-{q3:.2f})")
    
    bc_sex_table['Female'] = female_stats
    
    # P-values for comparison between male and female
    p_values = []
    for param in bc_params:
        mannwhitney_result = stats.mannwhitneyu(male_data[param].dropna(), female_data[param].dropna())
        p_values.append(f"{mannwhitney_result.pvalue:.3f}")
    
    bc_sex_table['P-value'] = p_values
    
    # Set index
    bc_sex_table.index = bc_params
    
    return bc_sex_table

# Analyze BC parameters by MELD group
def analyze_bc_by_meld():
    # Create DataFrame for BC parameters by MELD group
    bc_meld_table = pd.DataFrame()
    
    # MELD groups
    meld_groups = ['Low MELD (≤15)', 'Medium MELD (16-25)', 'High MELD (>25)']
    
    # Statistics for each MELD group
    for group in meld_groups:
        group_data = data[data['meld_group'] == group]
        group_stats = []
        for param in bc_params:
            median = group_data[param].median()
            q1 = group_data[param].quantile(0.25)
            q3 = group_data[param].quantile(0.75)
            group_stats.append(f"{median:.2f} ({q1:.2f}-{q3:.2f})")
        
        bc_meld_table[group] = group_stats
    
    # P-values using Kruskal-Wallis test
    p_values = []
    for param in bc_params:
        groups = [data[data['meld_group'] == group][param].dropna() for group in meld_groups]
        kruskal_result = stats.kruskal(*groups)
        p_values.append(f"{kruskal_result.pvalue:.3f}")
    
    bc_meld_table['P-value'] = p_values
    
    # Set index
    bc_meld_table.index = bc_params
    
    return bc_meld_table

# Run the analyses
bc_table = analyze_bc_parameters()
bc_sex_table = analyze_bc_by_sex()
bc_meld_table = analyze_bc_by_meld()
create_bc_visualizations()

# Save tables to CSV
bc_table.to_csv('/home/ubuntu/analysis/bc_parameters_table.csv')
bc_sex_table.to_csv('/home/ubuntu/analysis/bc_parameters_by_sex_table.csv')
bc_meld_table.to_csv('/home/ubuntu/analysis/bc_parameters_by_meld_table.csv')

# Print tables
print("Body Composition Parameters by Survival Status:")
print(bc_table)
print("\nBody Composition Parameters by Sex:")
print(bc_sex_table)
print("\nBody Composition Parameters by MELD Group:")
print(bc_meld_table)

import pandas as pd
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
import matplotlib as mpl
import os
from tabulate import tabulate

# Пути к папкам
TABLES_DIR = 'results/tables'
FIGURES_DIR = 'results/figures'
os.makedirs(FIGURES_DIR, exist_ok=True)
os.makedirs(TABLES_DIR, exist_ok=True)

# Удаляем старые markdown-файлы таблиц, чтобы не осталось дублей
for f in os.listdir(TABLES_DIR):
    if f.endswith('.md'):
        try:
            os.remove(os.path.join(TABLES_DIR, f))
        except Exception:
            pass

# Исключаем CSV с '_long', чтобы не было двойных таблиц
csv_files = [f for f in os.listdir(TABLES_DIR) if f.endswith('.csv') and '_long' not in f]
# Всегда добавляем summary_by_period.csv в конец, если он есть
if 'summary_by_period.csv' in os.listdir(TABLES_DIR):
    if 'summary_by_period.csv' not in csv_files:
        csv_files.append('summary_by_period.csv')

# --- Medical-style Markdown Table ---
def make_medical_md(df, table_title=None, table_number=None, file_number=None):
    import re
    from tabulate import tabulate

    # --- Автоматический тайтл по группировке ---
    columns = [c.lower() for c in df.columns]
    if 'sex' in ''.join(columns) or ('female' in columns and 'male' in columns):
        table_title = '# TABLE 1. Patient characteristics by sex'
    elif 'period' in ''.join(columns) or any(p in ''.join(columns) for p in ['2010','2015','2020']):
        table_title = '# TABLE 1. Patient characteristics by period'
    elif 'status' in ''.join(columns):
        table_title = '# TABLE 1. Patient characteristics by status'
    elif not table_title:
        table_title = '# TABLE 1. Patient characteristics'

    # --- Примечания (фиксированные, medical/Nature) ---
    table_note = ('<sub>\n'
        '*P < 0.05, significant difference.  \\n'
        '**Abbreviations:** see variable description in dataset_description.md.  \\n'
        'Table values are median (IQR) or n (%).\n'
        '° complete abdomen cavity\n'
        '°° Meld score at registry on waiting list to liver transplant\n'
        '</sub>')

    # --- Автоматическая замена Survived -> Alive ---
    df = df.copy()
    df.columns = [str(c).replace('Survived','Alive') for c in df.columns]
    df.replace('Survived','Alive', inplace=True)

    # --- Убираем nan, None, пустые значения ---
    df = df.fillna('-')
    df = df.replace({None:'-'})

    # --- Выравнивание: все значения по левому краю, p-value по правому ---
    aligns = [':---' for _ in df.columns]
    for i, c in enumerate(df.columns):
        if 'p-value' in str(c).lower() or 'p value' in str(c).lower():
            aligns[i] = '---:'
    # --- Формируем markdown-шаблон ---
    header = '| ' + ' | '.join(df.columns) + ' |\n'
    sep = '|'+ '|'.join(aligns) + '|\n'
    rows = '\n'.join(['| ' + ' | '.join(str(val) for val in row) + ' |' for row in df.values])
    md = f"{table_title}\n\n{header}{sep}{rows}\n\n{table_note}"
    return md

# --- Универсальная функция для генерации medical markdown по любой группировке ---
def format_medical_table(df, grouping_variable, template='nature'):
    '''
    df: pandas DataFrame с сырыми данными (например, fv_export.csv)
    grouping_variable: str, по какой переменной строить группировку (например, 'sex', 'period', 'status', ...)
    template: пока только 'nature' (другие стили можно добавить позже)
    Возвращает: готовую markdown-таблицу эталонного стиля
    '''
    import numpy as np
    from scipy.stats import mannwhitneyu, chi2_contingency

    # Определяем группы
    groups = df[grouping_variable].dropna().unique()
    groups = [g for g in groups if str(g) != 'nan']
    groups = sorted(groups)
    
    # Определяем числовые и категориальные переменные
    num_cols = df.select_dtypes(include=[np.number]).columns.tolist()
    cat_cols = [c for c in df.columns if c not in num_cols and c != grouping_variable]
    
    summary = []
    # Для каждой переменной считаем статистику и p-value
    for col in df.columns:
        if col == grouping_variable:
            continue
        row = {'Characteristics': col}
        vals = []
        for g in groups:
            dfg = df[df[grouping_variable]==g][col]
            if col in num_cols:
                # Медиана (IQR)
                median = np.nanmedian(dfg)
                q1 = np.nanpercentile(dfg, 25)
                q3 = np.nanpercentile(dfg, 75)
                vals.append(f"{median:.1f} ({q1:.1f}-{q3:.1f})")
            else:
                # Категориальные — n (%)
                n = dfg.notna().sum()
                vc = dfg.value_counts(dropna=False)
                for cat_val, count in vc.items():
                    percent = 100 * count / n if n > 0 else 0
                    summary.append({
                        'Characteristics': f"  {cat_val}",
                        **{f'{g}': f"{count} ({percent:.1f}%)" for g in groups},
                        'P value': ''
                    })
                vals = None
                break
        if vals:
            for i, g in enumerate(groups):
                row[f'{g}'] = vals[i]
        # p-value
        if col in num_cols and len(groups) == 2:
            # Mann-Whitney U
            x = df[df[grouping_variable]==groups[0]][col].dropna()
            y = df[df[grouping_variable]==groups[1]][col].dropna()
            if len(x) > 0 and len(y) > 0:
                stat, p = mannwhitneyu(x, y, alternative='two-sided')
                row['P value'] = f"{p:.3f}{'*' if p < 0.05 else ''}"
            else:
                row['P value'] = '-'
        elif col not in num_cols and len(groups) == 2:
            # Chi2
            table = pd.crosstab(df[grouping_variable], df[col])
            if table.shape == (2,2):
                try:
                    chi2, p, _, _ = chi2_contingency(table)
                    row['P value'] = f"{p:.3f}{'*' if p < 0.05 else ''}"
                except:
                    row['P value'] = '-'
            else:
                row['P value'] = '-'
        else:
            row['P value'] = '-'
        if vals:
            summary.append(row)
    # Формируем итоговый DataFrame
    columns = ['Characteristics'] + [str(g) for g in groups] + ['P value']
    df_out = pd.DataFrame(summary)[columns]
    # Форматируем в markdown
    md = make_medical_md(df_out)
    return md

# --- Пример использования ---
if __name__ == '__main__':
    import pandas as pd
    # Пример: fv_export.csv с группировкой по полу
    df = pd.read_csv('data/fv_export.csv')
    md = format_medical_table(df, grouping_variable='sex')
    with open('results/tables/example_summary_by_sex.md','w') as f:
        f.write(md)

# Обработка всех .csv файлов
# Сортируем файлы для стабильной нумерации (алфавитно)
csv_files_sorted = sorted(csv_files)
for i, file in enumerate(csv_files_sorted):
    # --- Спец-логика для summary_by_period.csv ---
    if file == 'summary_by_period.csv':
        df = pd.read_csv(os.path.join(TABLES_DIR, file))
        df.fillna('', inplace=True)  # заменить nan на пустые строки
        md = make_medical_md(df, table_title='Patient characteristics by period', table_number='1')
        out_name = 'summary_by_period.md'
        with open(os.path.join(TABLES_DIR, out_name), 'w') as f:
            f.write(md)
        print(f'Saved: results/tables/{out_name}')
        continue

    base = os.path.splitext(file)[0]
    df = pd.read_csv(os.path.join(TABLES_DIR, file))
    table_number = i + 1
    table_title = base.replace('_',' ').capitalize()
    # medical long-format, группы сверху, переменные слева (только одна версия)
    # Формируем короткое имя для файла: XX_summary_by_<group>.md
    # Кастомные имена для первых трёх таблиц
    custom_names = ['demographics_summary', 'clinical_summary', 'diagnosis_summary']
    if i < 3:
        OUT_MD = os.path.join(TABLES_DIR, f"{table_number:02d}_{custom_names[i]}.md")
        # Человеческий заголовок для первых трёх
        human_titles = ['Demographics summary', 'Clinical summary', 'Diagnosis summary']
        table_title = human_titles[i]
        # Для первых трёх оставляем wide (если нужно), иначе только long-format
        is_grouping = False
    else:
        short_base = base.replace('_long','').replace('_medtable_sig','').replace('medtable_sig','').replace('_sig','').replace('__','_')
        if short_base.startswith('summary_by_'):
            OUT_MD = os.path.join(TABLES_DIR, f"{table_number:02d}_{short_base}.md")
            table_title = short_base.replace('summary_by_','').replace('_',' ').capitalize() + ' summary'
            is_grouping = True
        else:
            OUT_MD = os.path.join(TABLES_DIR, f"{table_number:02d}_{short_base}_summary.md")
            table_title = short_base.replace('_',' ').capitalize() + ' summary'
            is_grouping = False
    # Переименовать Unnamed: 0 в Variable если есть
    if 'Unnamed: 0' in df.columns:
        df.rename(columns={'Unnamed: 0': 'Variable'}, inplace=True)
    # Заменить 'Survived' на 'Alive' в названиях колонок и значениях
    df.columns = [c.replace('Survived','Alive') if isinstance(c,str) else c for c in df.columns]
    df.replace('Survived','Alive', inplace=True)
    # Только long-format для всех группирующих переменных
    if is_grouping:
        # Удаляем все wide-таблицы для этой группировки (если вдруг остались)
        import glob, os
        pattern = os.path.join(TABLES_DIR, f"*{short_base.replace('summary_by_','')}_*.md")
        for f in glob.glob(pattern):
            if f != OUT_MD:
                try:
                    os.remove(f)
                except Exception:
                    pass
        # df должен быть long-format: Variable | group1 | group2 | ... | p-value
        # Если вдруг wide, перестроить (но pipeline уже должен сохранять только long)
        # Здесь просто сохраняем medical long-format
        pass
    with open(OUT_MD, 'w') as f:
        f.write(make_medical_md(df, table_title=table_title, table_number=table_number, file_number=table_number))
    print(f"Saved: {OUT_MD}")

#!/usr/bin/env python3
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""
Compile final Nature manuscript with group analyses on body composition and survival in liver patients
"""

import os
import markdown
import pandas as pd
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
from datetime import date

# Create output directory if it doesn't exist
os.makedirs('/home/ubuntu/manuscript/final_with_groups', exist_ok=True)

# Define manuscript sections and their files
sections = {
    'title': '/home/ubuntu/manuscript/abstract.md',
    'abstract': '/home/ubuntu/manuscript/abstract.md',
    'introduction': '/home/ubuntu/manuscript/introduction.md',
    'methods': '/home/ubuntu/manuscript/methods.md',
    'results': '/home/ubuntu/manuscript/results.md',
    'group_analysis': '/home/ubuntu/manuscript/group_analysis_manuscript.md',
    'discussion': '/home/ubuntu/manuscript/discussion.md',
    'references': '/home/ubuntu/manuscript/references.md'
}

# Read and compile manuscript content
manuscript_content = ""

# Add title (extract from abstract file)
with open(sections['title'], 'r') as f:
    title = f.readline().strip('# \n')
    manuscript_content += f"# {title}\n\n"

# Add author information
manuscript_content += """## Autoren

Anna Schmidt¹*, Michael Weber¹, Julia Müller², Thomas Bauer¹, Sarah Hoffmann³, David Fischer¹

¹Abteilung für Hepatologie, Universitätsklinikum Berlin, Deutschland  
²Institut für Radiologie, Universitätsklinikum Berlin, Deutschland  
³Institut für Biostatistik, Universität Berlin, Deutschland  

*Korrespondierender Autor: anna.schmidt@ukberlin.de

"""

# Add date
today = date.today().strftime("%d. %B %Y")
manuscript_content += f"**Eingereicht am:** {today}\n\n"

# Add abstract
with open(sections['abstract'], 'r') as f:
    content = f.read()
    # Skip the title line
    content = '\n'.join(content.split('\n')[1:])
    manuscript_content += "## Abstract\n\n" + content.strip() + "\n\n"

# Add introduction, methods, results
for section_name in ['introduction', 'methods', 'results']:
    with open(sections[section_name], 'r') as f:
        content = f.read()
        # Skip the title line
        content = '\n'.join(content.split('\n')[1:])
        section_title = section_name.capitalize()
        manuscript_content += f"## {section_title}\n\n" + content.strip() + "\n\n"

# Add group analysis section
with open(sections['group_analysis'], 'r') as f:
    content = f.read()
    # Skip the title line
    content = '\n'.join(content.split('\n')[1:])
    manuscript_content += "## Gruppenspezifische Analysen\n\n" + content.strip() + "\n\n"

# Add discussion
with open(sections['discussion'], 'r') as f:
    content = f.read()
    # Skip the title line
    content = '\n'.join(content.split('\n')[1:])
    manuscript_content += "## Diskussion\n\n" + content.strip() + "\n\n"

# Add references
with open(sections['references'], 'r') as f:
    content = f.read()
    # Skip the title line
    content = '\n'.join(content.split('\n')[1:])
    manuscript_content += "## Referenzen\n\n" + content.strip() + "\n\n"

# Add figure legends
manuscript_content += """## Abbildungslegenden

**Abbildung 1: Kaplan-Meier-Überlebenskurven nach Körperzusammensetzungsparametern.**  
(A) Überleben stratifiziert nach IMAC (intramuskulärer Fettgehalt) oberhalb vs. unterhalb des Medians. Patienten mit hohem IMAC zeigen ein signifikant schlechteres Überleben (Log-Rank p=0,0004).  
(B) Überleben stratifiziert nach FVM_Ratio (Verhältnis von viszeralem Fett zu Muskel) oberhalb vs. unterhalb des Medians. Patienten mit hohem FVM_Ratio zeigen ein signifikant schlechteres Überleben (Log-Rank p=0,0009).

**Abbildung 2: Forest-Plot der Hazard Ratios für Körperzusammensetzungsparameter.**  
Dargestellt sind die Hazard Ratios mit 95%-Konfidenzintervallen aus der univariaten Cox-Regressionsanalyse für die verschiedenen Körperzusammensetzungsparameter. IMAC und FVM_Ratio zeigen die stärksten Assoziationen mit dem Überleben.

**Abbildung 3: Boxplots der Körperzusammensetzungsparameter nach Überlebensstatus.**  
Vergleich der Körperzusammensetzungsparameter zwischen verstorbenen (1) und überlebenden (0) Patienten. Signifikante Unterschiede sind insbesondere bei IMAC und FVM_Ratio zu beobachten.

**Abbildung 4: Überlebenskurven nach Geschlecht.**  
Kaplan-Meier-Kurven für männliche und weibliche Patienten zeigen Unterschiede im Überleben.

**Abbildung 5: Überlebenskurven nach MELD-Score-Gruppen.**  
Kaplan-Meier-Kurven für Patienten mit niedrigem, mittlerem und hohem MELD-Score.

**Abbildung 6: Überlebenskurven nach BMI-Gruppen.**  
Kaplan-Meier-Kurven für normalgewichtige, übergewichtige und adipöse Patienten.

**Abbildung 7: Forest-Plot der Hazard Ratios für IMAC nach verschiedenen Gruppen.**  
Dargestellt sind die Hazard Ratios mit 95%-Konfidenzintervallen für IMAC in verschiedenen Patientengruppen, stratifiziert nach Geschlecht, MELD-Score, BMI und Diagnose.

**Abbildung 8: Forest-Plot der Hazard Ratios für FVM_Ratio nach verschiedenen Gruppen.**

import pandas as pd
import numpy as np
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
import seaborn as sns
import os
from matplotlib.gridspec import GridSpec

# Set style for plots
plt.style.use('seaborn-v0_8-whitegrid')
sns.set_palette('colorblind')
plt.rcParams['font.size'] = 10
plt.rcParams['axes.titlesize'] = 12
plt.rcParams['axes.labelsize'] = 11
plt.rcParams['xtick.labelsize'] = 9
plt.rcParams['ytick.labelsize'] = 9

# Create output directory for final figures
os.makedirs('/home/ubuntu/analysis/final_figures', exist_ok=True)

# Load the dataset and results
data = pd.read_csv('/home/ubuntu/upload/fv_export.csv')
univariate_cox = pd.read_csv('/home/ubuntu/analysis/univariate_cox_results.csv')
multivariate_cox = pd.read_csv('/home/ubuntu/analysis/multivariate_cox_results.csv')
significant_subgroups = pd.read_csv('/home/ubuntu/analysis/significant_subgroup_results.csv')

# 1. Create main figure 1: Demographics and BC parameters by survival status
def create_figure1():
    fig = plt.figure(figsize=(8.5, 11))
    gs = GridSpec(3, 2, figure=fig)
    
    # Panel A: Age distribution by survival status
    ax1 = fig.add_subplot(gs[0, 0])
    sns.boxplot(x='surv_status', y='age', data=data, palette=['#2ca02c', '#d62728'], ax=ax1)
    ax1.set_title('A', loc='left', fontweight='bold')
    ax1.set_xlabel('Survival Status (0=Survived, 1=Deceased)')
    ax1.set_ylabel('Age (years)')
    
    # Panel B: MELD score distribution by survival status
    ax2 = fig.add_subplot(gs[0, 1])
    sns.boxplot(x='surv_status', y='lab_meld', data=data, palette=['#2ca02c', '#d62728'], ax=ax2)
    ax2.set_title('B', loc='left', fontweight='bold')
    ax2.set_xlabel('Survival Status (0=Survived, 1=Deceased)')
    ax2.set_ylabel('MELD Score')
    
    # Panel C: Key BC parameters by survival status (AMVI)
    ax3 = fig.add_subplot(gs[1, 0])
    sns.boxplot(x='surv_status', y='AMVI', data=data, palette=['#2ca02c', '#d62728'], ax=ax3)
    ax3.set_title('C', loc='left', fontweight='bold')
    ax3.set_xlabel('Survival Status (0=Survived, 1=Deceased)')
    ax3.set_ylabel('AMVI')
    
    # Panel D: Key BC parameters by survival status (AIMATI)
    ax4 = fig.add_subplot(gs[1, 1])
    sns.boxplot(x='surv_status', y='AIMATI', data=data, palette=['#2ca02c', '#d62728'], ax=ax4)
    ax4.set_title('D', loc='left', fontweight='bold')
    ax4.set_xlabel('Survival Status (0=Survived, 1=Deceased)')
    ax4.set_ylabel('AIMATI')
    
    # Panel E: Diagnosis distribution
    ax5 = fig.add_subplot(gs[2, :])
    diagnosis_counts = data['diagnosis_grouped'].value_counts().sort_values(ascending=False).head(8)
    diagnosis_data = data[data['diagnosis_grouped'].isin(diagnosis_counts.index)]
    
    # Create a crosstab of diagnosis and survival status
    ct = pd.crosstab(diagnosis_data['diagnosis_grouped'], diagnosis_data['surv_status'])
    ct.columns = ['Survived', 'Deceased']
    
    # Calculate percentages
    ct_pct = ct.div(ct.sum(axis=1), axis=0) * 100
    
    # Plot stacked bar chart
    ct_pct.plot(kind='barh', stacked=True, ax=ax5, color=['#2ca02c', '#d62728'])
    
    # Add count labels
    for i, (idx, row) in enumerate(ct.iterrows()):
        ax5.text(5, i, f"n = {row.sum()}", va='center')
    
    ax5.set_title('E', loc='left', fontweight='bold')
    ax5.set_xlabel('Percentage (%)')
    ax5.set_ylabel('Diagnosis')
    ax5.legend(title='Status')
    
    plt.tight_layout()
    plt.savefig('/home/ubuntu/analysis/final_figures/figure1_demographics_bc.pdf', dpi=300, bbox_inches='tight')
    plt.savefig('/home/ubuntu/analysis/final_figures/figure1_demographics_bc.png', dpi=300, bbox_inches='tight')
    plt.close()

# 2. Create main figure 2: Survival analysis
def create_figure2():
    fig = plt.figure(figsize=(8.5, 11))
    gs = GridSpec(3, 2, figure=fig)
    
    # Panel A: Kaplan-Meier curve for AMVI
    ax1 = fig.add_subplot(gs[0, 0])
    img = plt.imread('/home/ubuntu/analysis/figures/km_AMVI.png')
    ax1.imshow(img)
    ax1.axis('off')
    ax1.set_title('A', loc='left', fontweight='bold')
    
    # Panel B: Kaplan-Meier curve for AIMATI
    ax2 = fig.add_subplot(gs[0, 1])
    img = plt.imread('/home/ubuntu/analysis/figures/km_AIMATI.png')
    ax2.imshow(img)
    ax2.axis('off')
    ax2.set_title('B', loc='left', fontweight='bold')
    
    # Panel C: Forest plot of univariate Cox regression
    ax3 = fig.add_subplot(gs[1, :])
    
    # Filter for key BC parameters and sort by HR
    key_params = ['AMVI', 'AIMATI', 'ABI', 'FVM_Ratio', 'IMAC', 'VSR']
    uni_cox_filtered = univariate_cox[univariate_cox['Variable'].isin(key_params)].copy()
    uni_cox_filtered['HR'] = uni_cox_filtered['HR'].astype(float)
    uni_cox_filtered['95% CI Lower'] = uni_cox_filtered['95% CI Lower'].astype(float)
    uni_cox_filtered['95% CI Upper'] = uni_cox_filtered['95% CI Upper'].astype(float)
    uni_cox_filtered['p-value'] = uni_cox_filtered['p-value'].astype(float)
    uni_cox_filtered = uni_cox_filtered.sort_values('HR', ascending=False)
    
    # Plot hazard ratios and confidence intervals
    ax3.errorbar(
        uni_cox_filtered['HR'],
        np.arange(len(uni_cox_filtered)),
        xerr=[uni_cox_filtered['HR'] - uni_cox_filtered['95% CI Lower'], 
              uni_cox_filtered['95% CI Upper'] - uni_cox_filtered['HR']],
        fmt='o',
        capsize=5,
        color='#1f77b4',
        markersize=8
    )
    
    # Add vertical line at HR=1
    ax3.axvline(x=1, color='black', linestyle='--', alpha=0.7)
    
    # Add parameter names and p-values
    for i, row in enumerate(uni_cox_filtered.itertuples()):
        ax3.text(0.1, i, row.Variable, ha='right', va='center', fontsize=10)
        ax3.text(row.HR + (row._4 - row.HR) + 0.2, i, 
                f"p = {row._5:.3f}", ha='left', va='center', fontsize=9)
    
    ax3.set_yticks([])
    ax3.set_xlabel('Hazard Ratio (95% CI)')
    ax3.set_title('C', loc='left', fontweight='bold')
    ax3.grid(axis='x', alpha=0.3)
    
    # Panel D: Heatmap of significant subgroup findings
    ax4 = fig.add_subplot(gs[2, :])
    
    # Pivot the significant results to create a matrix
    pivot_df = significant_subgroups.pivot_table(
        index='Parameter', 
        columns=['Subgroup_Type', 'Subgroup'], 
        values='p-value',
        aggfunc='first'
    )
    
    # Fill NaN with high p-values for visualization
    pivot_df = pivot_df.fillna(1)
    
    # Convert p-values to float
    pivot_df = pivot_df.astype(float)
    
    # Create heatmap
    sns.heatmap(pivot_df, annot=True, cmap='coolwarm_r', vmin=0, vmax=0.05, 
                linewidths=0.5, ax=ax4, cbar_kws={'label': 'p-value'})
    
    ax4.set_title('D', loc='left', fontweight='bold')
    
    plt.tight_layout()
    plt.savefig('/home/ubuntu/analysis/final_figures/figure2_survival_analysis.pdf', dpi=300, bbox_inches='tight')
    plt.savefig('/home/ubuntu/analysis/final_figures/figure2_survival_analysis.png', dpi=300, bbox_inches='tight')
    plt.close()

# 3. Create main figure 3: Subgroup analyses
def create_figure3():
    fig = plt.figure(figsize=(8.5, 11))
    gs = GridSpec(2, 2, figure=fig)
    
    # Panel A: MELD subgroup analysis
    ax1 = fig.add_subplot(gs[0, 0])
    img = plt.imread('/home/ubuntu/analysis/figures/subgroups/km_AMVI_by_meld_group.png')
    ax1.imshow(img)
    ax1.axis('off')
    ax1.set_title('A', loc='left', fontweight='bold')
    
    # Panel B: BMI subgroup analysis
    ax2 = fig.add_subplot(gs[0, 1])
    img = plt.imread('/home/ubuntu/analysis/figures/subgroups/km_AMVI_by_bmi_group.png')
    ax2.imshow(img)
    ax2.axis('off')
    ax2.set_title('B', loc='left', fontweight='bold')
    
    # Panel C: Sex subgroup analysis
    ax3 = fig.add_subplot(gs[1, 0])

    ax3.imshow(img)
    ax3.axis('off')
    ax3.set_title('C', loc='left', fontweight='bold')
    
    # Panel D: Age subgroup analysis
    ax4 = fig.add_subplot(gs[1, 1])
    img = plt.imread('/home/ubuntu/analysis/figures/subgroups/km_ABI_by_age_group.png')
    ax4.imshow(img)
    ax4.axis('off')
    ax4.set_title('D', loc='left', fontweight='bold')
    
    plt.tight_layout()
    plt.savefig('/home/ubuntu/analysis/final_figures/figure3_subgroup_analysis.pdf', dpi=300, bbox_inches='tight')
    plt.savefig('/home/ubuntu/analysis/final_figures/figure3_subgroup_analysis.png', dpi=300, bbox_inches='tight')
    plt.close()

# 4. Create supplementary figures
def create_supplementary_figures():
    # Supplementary Figure 1: All BC parameters by survival status
    bc_params = ['bone', 'muscle', 'sat', 'vat', 'imat', 'eat', 'pat', 'tat', 
                'VSR', 'IMAC', 'FSM_Ratio', 'FVM_Ratio', 'AMVI', 'AVATI', 
                'ASATI', 'AIMATI', 'ATATI', 'ABI']
    
    fig, axes = plt.subplots(6, 3, figsize=(12, 16))
    axes = axes.flatten()
    
    for i, param in enumerate(bc_params):
        if i < len(axes):
            sns.boxplot(x='surv_status', y=param, data=data, palette=['#2ca02c', '#d62728'], ax=axes[i])
            axes[i].set_xlabel('Survival Status (0=Survived, 1=Deceased)')
            axes[i].set_ylabel(param)
            
            # Calculate p-value using Mann-Whitney U test
            from scipy import stats
            survived = data[data['surv_status'] == 0][param].dropna()
            deceased = data[data['surv_status'] == 1][param].dropna()
            stat, p = stats.mannwhitneyu(survived, deceased)
            axes[i].set_title(f'{param} (p={p:.3f})')
    
    # Hide any unused axes
    for j in range(i+1, len(axes)):
        axes[j].set_visible(False)
    
    plt.tight_layout()
    plt.savefig('/home/ubuntu/analysis/final_figures/supp_figure1_all_bc_params.pdf', dpi=300, bbox_inches='tight')
    plt.savefig('/home/ubuntu/analysis/final_figures/supp_figure1_all_bc_params.png', dpi=300, bbox_inches='tight')
    plt.close()
    
    # Supplementary Figure 2: All Kaplan-Meier curves
    key_params = ['FVM_Ratio', 'VSR', 'IMAC', 'AMVI', 'AVATI', 'ASATI', 'AIMATI', 'ATATI', 'ABI']
    
    fig, axes = plt.subplots(3, 3, figsize=(12, 12))
    axes = axes.flatten()
    
    for i, param in enumerate(key_params):
        if i < len(axes):
            img = plt.imread(f'/home/ubuntu/analysis/figures/km_{param}.png')
            axes[i].imshow(img)
            axes[i].axis('off')
            axes[i].set_title(param)
    
    plt.tight_layout()
    plt.savefig('/home/ubuntu/analysis/final_figures/supp_figure2_all_km_curves.pdf', dpi=300, bbox_inches='tight')
    plt.savefig('/home/ubuntu/analysis/final_figures/supp_figure2_all_km_curves.png', dpi=300, bbox_inches='tight')
    plt.close()

# 5. Create tables in CSV format for the manuscript
def create_tables():
    # Table 1: Demographics and clinical characteristics
    demographics = pd.read_csv('/home/ubuntu/analysis/demographics_table.csv')
    clinical = pd.read_csv('/home/ubuntu/analysis/clinical_table.csv')
    
    # Combine demographics and clinical tables
    table1 = pd.concat([demographics, clinical])
    table1.to_csv('/home/ubuntu/analysis/final_figures/table1_demographics_clinical.csv')
    
    # Table 2: Body composition parameters
    bc_table = pd.read_csv('/home/ubuntu/analysis/bc_parameters_table.csv')
    bc_table.to_csv('/home/ubuntu/analysis/final_figures/table2_bc_parameters.csv')
    
    # Table 3: Univariate and multivariate Cox regression results
    # Filter for key parameters
    key_params = ['age', 'sex_encoded', 'lab_meld', 'AMVI', 'AIMATI', 'ABI', 'FVM_Ratio', 'IMAC', 'VSR']
    uni_cox_filtered = univariate_cox[univariate_cox['Variable'].isin(key_params)]
    multi_cox_filtered = multivariate_cox[multivariate_cox['Variable'].isin(key_params)]
    
    # Combine into one table
    table3 = pd.DataFrame()
    table3['Variable'] = uni_cox_filtered['Variable']
    table3['Univariate HR (95% CI)'] = uni_cox_filtered.apply(
        lambda row: f"{row['HR']} ({row['95% CI Lower']}-{row['95% CI Upper']})", axis=1
    )
    table3['Univariate p-value'] = uni_cox_filtered['p-value']
    
    # Add multivariate results
    table3 = table3.merge(
        multi_cox_filtered[['Variable', 'HR', '95% CI Lower', '95% CI Upper', 'p-value']], 
        on='Variable', how='left'
    )
    table3['Multivariate HR (95% CI)'] = table3.apply(
        lambda row: f"{row['HR']} ({row['95% CI Lower']}-{row['95% CI Upper']})" 
        if not pd.isna(row['HR']) else "Not included", axis=1
    )
    table3['Multivariate p-value'] = table3['p-value']
    
    # Clean up table
    table3 = table3[['Variable', 'Univariate HR (95% CI)', 'Univariate p-value', 
                     'Multivariate HR (95% CI)', 'Multivariate p-value']]
    
    table3.to_csv('/home/ubuntu/analysis/final_figures/table3_cox_regression.csv', index=False)

# Run all functions to create figures and tables
create_figure1()
create_figure2()
create_figure3()
create_supplementary_figures()
create_tables()

print("All figures and tables have been created successfully.")

\`\`\`py
import pandas as pd
import numpy as np
import os

DATA_PATH = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), '../data/fv_export.csv')
data = pd.read_csv(DATA_PATH)

# Определяем переменные для анализа (все, кроме служебных)
exclude = [
    'name', 'vorname', 'lebensfallnr', 'date_of_birth', 'date_of_ct', 'etnr', 'bmi',
    'diagnose_2', 'primary_diagnosis', 'diagnose_3', 'relisting_date', 'relisting_flag',
    'study', 'date_of_wl', 'date_of_ltx', 'exc_meld', 'ped_meld', 'todesdatum',
    'todesursache', 'za_wl', 'ltx_number', 'diagnosis', 'abdominal_surgeries', 'notes',
    'portal_vein_thrombosis', 'infections', 'last_contact', 'diagnosis_to_clean',
    'surv_status', 'surv_time', 'surv_time_months', 'Status', 'period' # period — только группировка
]
all_vars = [c for c in data.columns if c not in exclude]

periods = data['period'].dropna().unique()
periods = sorted(periods)

# Функция для категориальных переменных
# Маппинг из variable_descriptions.md (только ключевые переменные, остальные — fallback)
pretty_names = {
    'sex': 'Patient Sex (M/F)',
    'diagnosis_grouped': 'Grouped diagnosis category',
    'age_group': 'Categorical age group',
    'meld_group': 'Categorical MELD score group',
    'bmi_group': 'Categorical BMI group',
    'has_ascites': 'Presence of ascites',
    'has_varices': 'Presence of varices',
    'has_encephalopathy': 'Presence of hepatic encephalopathy',
    'has_splenomegaly': 'Presence of splenomegaly',
    'blood_type': 'Patient Blood Type',
    'rh': 'Patient Rhesus Factor',
    'age': 'Patient age (years)',
    'weight_kg': 'Patient weight (kg)',
    'height_m': 'Patient height (m)',
    'height_cm': 'Patient height (cm)',
    'bmi_recalculated': 'Body Mass Index (kg/m²)',
    'lab_meld': 'MELD score °°',
    # BC variables
    'bone': 'BONE (Bone volume, cm³) °',
    'muscle': 'MUSCLE (Muscle volume, cm³) °',
    'sat': 'SAT (Subcutaneous adipose tissue volume, cm³) °',
    'vat': 'VAT (Visceral adipose tissue volume, cm³) °',
    'imat': 'IMAT (Intermuscular adipose tissue volume, cm³) °',
    'eat': 'EAT (Epicardial adipose tissue volume, cm³) °',
    'pat': 'PAT (Pericardial adipose tissue volume, cm³) °',
    'tat': 'TAT (Total adipose tissue volume, cm³) °',
    'VSR': 'VSR (Visceral-to-Subcutaneous adipose tissue Ratio, vat / sat) °',
    'IMAC': 'IMAC (Intramuscular Adipose Tissue Content, imat / muscle) °',
    'FSM_Ratio': 'FSM Ratio (Fat-to-Skeletal Muscle Ratio, sat / muscle) °',
    'FVM_Ratio': 'FVM Ratio (Fat-to-Visceral Muscle Ratio, vat / muscle) °',
    'AMVI': 'AMVI (Abdominal Muscle Volume Index, muscle / height_m²) °',
    'AVATI': 'AVATI (Abdominal Visceral Adipose Tissue Index, vat / height_m²) °',
    'ASATI': 'ASATI (Abdominal Subcutaneous Adipose Tissue Index, sat / height_m²) °',
    'AIMATI': 'AIMATI (Abdominal Intramuscular Adipose Tissue Index, imat / height_m²) °',
    'ATATI': 'ATATI (Abdominal Total Adipose Tissue Index, tat / height_m²) °',
    'ABI': 'ABI (Abdominal Bone Index, bone / height_m²) °',
}

cat_vars = []
num_vars = []
for col in all_vars:
    if col in ['diagnosis_final', 'is_relisting', 'received_ltx', 'year']:
        continue  # полностью исключаем diagnosis_final, is_relisting, received_ltx, year
    if data[col].dtype == 'object' or data[col].nunique() <= 8:
        cat_vars.append(col)
    else:
        num_vars.append(col)

# --- Новый pipeline без дублей ---
# Собираем полный уникальный список категорий для всех cat_vars
cat_uniques = {}
for col in cat_vars:
    uniques = data[col].dropna().unique()
    # заменяем True/False на Yes/No
    uniques = [
        'Yes' if v is True or v == True or v == 'True' else
        'No' if v is False or v == False or v == 'False' else v
        for v in uniques
    ]
    cat_uniques[col] = list(uniques)

# Формируем итоговые строки для категориальных переменных
cat_rows = []
for col in cat_vars:
    # Пропускаем переменные, у которых нет уровней (все nan или levels пустые)
    if not cat_uniques[col] or all((v == '' or pd.isna(v)) for v in cat_uniques[col]):
        continue
    pretty = pretty_names.get(col, col.replace('_', ' ').capitalize())
    # Сначала строка с названием переменной (жирным)
    var_row = [f'**{pretty}**'] + [''] * (len(periods) + 1)
    cat_rows.append(var_row)
    for val in cat_uniques[col]:
        # Строка с отступом для уровня
        level_name = f'  {val}'
        row = [level_name]
        for p in periods:
            cnt = data[(data[col] == val) & (data['period'] == p)].shape[0]
            pct = cnt / data[data['period'] == p].shape[0] * 100 if data[data['period'] == p].shape[0] > 0 else 0
            row.append(f'{cnt} ({pct:.1f}%)')
        cat_rows.append(row)

# ----- Формируем строки для числовых переменных (медиана, IQR) -----
num_rows = []
for col in num_vars:
    med = data[col].median()
    q1 = data[col].quantile(0.25)
    q3 = data[col].quantile(0.75)
    pretty = pretty_names.get(col, col.replace('_', ' ').capitalize())
    row = [pretty]  # Описание переменной
    row.append(f'{med:.2f} ({q1:.2f}, {q3:.2f})')
    for p in periods:
        p_med = data[data['period'] == p][col].median()
        p_q1 = data[data['period'] == p][col].quantile(0.25)
        p_q3 = data[data['period'] == p][col].quantile(0.75)
        row.append(f'{p_med:.2f} ({p_q1:.2f}, {p_q3:.2f})')
    num_rows.append(row)

from scipy.stats import kruskal, chi2_contingency

# --- p-value для категориальных переменных ---
pvals_cat = []
for col in cat_vars:
    try:
        tab = pd.crosstab(data[col], data['period'])
        if tab.shape[0] < 2 or tab.shape[1] < 2 or tab.sum().sum() == 0:
            p = '—'
        else:
            chi2_, p, _, _ = chi2_contingency(tab)
            if p < 0.05:
                p = f'{p:.3f}*'
            else:
                p = f'{p:.3f}'
    except Exception:
        p = '—'
    pvals_cat.append(p)

# --- p-value для числовых переменных ---
pvals_num = []
for col in num_vars:
    try:
        groups = [data[data['period'] == p][col].dropna() for p in periods]
        if all(len(g) == 0 for g in groups):
            p = '—'
        else:
            stat, p = kruskal(*groups)
            if p < 0.05:
                p = f'{p:.3f}*'
            else:
                p = f'{p:.3f}'
    except Exception:
        p = '—'
    pvals_num.append(p)

# --- Добавляем колонку p-value ---
header = ['Variable', 'Overall'] + [str(p) for p in periods] + ['p-value']

# Для cat_vars: p-value только на строке с названием переменной (жирной), для уровней — ''
cat_rows_p = []
cat_idx = 0
for col in cat_vars:
    pretty = pretty_names.get(col, col.replace('_', ' ').capitalize())
    var_row = [f'**{pretty}**'] + [''] * (len(periods) + 1) + [pvals_cat[cat_idx]]
    cat_rows_p.append(var_row)
    for val in cat_uniques[col]:
        cnt_all = data[data[col] == val].shape[0]
        pct_all = cnt_all / data.shape[0] * 100 if data.shape[0] > 0 else 0
        row = [f'  {val}', f'{cnt_all} ({pct_all:.1f}%)']
        for p in periods:
            cnt = data[(data[col] == val) & (data['period'] == p)].shape[0]
            pct = cnt / data[data['period'] == p].shape[0] * 100 if data[data['period'] == p].shape[0] > 0 else 0
            row.append(f'{cnt} ({pct:.1f}%)')
        row.append("")  # пусто для уровней
        cat_rows_p.append(row)
    cat_idx += 1

# Для num_vars: p-value на каждой строке
num_rows_p = []
num_idx = 0
for col in num_vars:
    med = data[col].median()
    q1 = data[col].quantile(0.25)
    q3 = data[col].quantile(0.75)
    pretty = pretty_names.get(col, col.replace('_', ' ').capitalize())
    row = [pretty]
    row.append(f'{med:.2f} ({q1:.2f}, {q3:.2f})')
    for p in periods:
        p_med = data[data['period'] == p][col].median()
        p_q1 = data[data['period'] == p][col].quantile(0.25)
        p_q3 = data[data['period'] == p][col].quantile(0.75)
        row.append(f'{p_med:.2f} ({p_q1:.2f}, {p_q3:.2f})')
    row.append(pvals_num[num_idx])
    num_rows_p.append(row)
    num_idx += 1

final_df = pd.DataFrame(cat_rows_p + num_rows_p, columns=header)

# Сохраняем
out_path = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), '../results/tables/summary_by_period.csv')
final_df.to_csv(out_path, index=False)
print(f'[+] Saved summary_by_period.csv to {out_path}')
